hit_points = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        let tags = self\n            .sql(&format!(\n                \"SELECT hex(tag) FROM monster_tags WHERE monster_slug = {} ORDER BY position;\",\n                sql_text(&slug)\n            ))?\n            .lines()\n            .map(decode_sql_text)\n            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;\n        Ok(Some(Monster {\n            slug,\n            name,\n            cr,\n            armor_class,\n            hit_points,\n            tags,\n        }))\n    }\n\n    fn create_item(&self, item: &Item) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM items WHERE slug = {};\",\n            sql_text(&item.slug)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO items(slug,name,type,rarity,cost_gp) VALUES ({},{},{},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&item.slug),\n            sql_text(&item.name),\n            sql_text(&item.kind),\n            sql_text(&item.rarity),\n            item.cost_gp\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn item(&self, slug: &str) -> Result<Option<Item>, ()> {\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(slug),hex(name),hex(type),hex(rarity),cost_gp FROM items WHERE slug = {};\",\n            sql_text(slug)\n        ))?;\n        let Some(fields) = row.trim().split_once('|') else {\n            return Ok(None);\n        };\n        let mut fields = std::iter::once(fields.0).chain(fields.1.split('|'));\n        let slug = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let name = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let kind = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let rarity = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let cost_gp = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        Ok(Some(Item {\n            slug,\n            name,\n            kind,\n            rarity,\n            cost_gp,\n        }))\n    }\n\n    /// Combat and authentication state predate the relational tables, so it is\n    /// stored together in one versioned BLOB.  Its deterministic encoding is\n    /// part of the persistence contract and must remain backward compatible.\n    fn load(&self) -> Result<(Sessions, Users), ()> {\n        let value = self.sql(\"SELECT hex(data) FROM game_state WHERE id = 1;\")?;\n        let value = value.trim();\n        if value.is_empty() {\n            return Ok((HashMap::new(), HashMap::new()));\n        }\n        decode_state(&decode_hex(value)?).ok_or(())\n    }\n\n    fn save(&self, sessions: &Sessions, users: &Users) -> Result<(), ()> {\n        let bytes = encode_state(sessions, users);\n        let statement = format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO game_state(id, data) VALUES (1, X'{}') ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET data = excluded.data;\",\n            hex(&bytes)\n        );\n        self.sql(&statement).map(|_| ())\n    }\n\n    fn sql(&self, statement: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n        let output = Command::new(\"sqlite3\")\n            .arg(&self.path)\n            .arg(statement)\n            .output()\n            .map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if output.status.success() {\n            String::from_utf8(output.stdout).map_err(|_| ())\n        } else {\n            Err(())\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn sql_text(value: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\"CAST(X'{}' AS TEXT)\", hex(value.as_bytes()))\n}\nfn decode_sql_text(value: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    String::from_utf8(decode_hex(value)?).map_err(|_| ())\n}\n\nfn put_u64(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: u64) {\n    out.extend_from_slice(&value.to_be_bytes());\n}\nfn put_i64(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: i64) {\n    out.extend_from_slice(&value.to_be_bytes());\n}\nfn put_string(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: &str) {\n    put_u64(out, value.len() as u64);\n    out.extend_from_slice(value.as_bytes());\n}\n\nfn encode_state(sessions: &Sessions, users: &Users) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2.to_vec();\n    let mut usernames = users.keys().collect::<Vec<_>>();\n    usernames.sort();\n    put_u64(&mut out, usernames.len() as u64);\n    for username in usernames {\n        put_string(&mut out, username);\n        put_string(&mut out, &users[username].password_hash);\n        put_string(&mut out, &users[username].role);\n    }\n    let mut ids = sessions.keys().collect::<Vec<_>>();\n    ids.sort();\n    put_u64(&mut out, ids.len() as u64);\n    for id in ids {\n        let session = &sessions[id];\n        put_string(&mut out, id);\n        put_i64(&mut out, session.round);\n        put_u64(&mut out, session.turn_index as u64);\n        put_u64(&mut out, session.order.len() as u64);\n        for combatant in &session.order {\n            put_string(&mut out, &combatant.name);\n            put_i64(&mut out, combatant.dex);\n            put_i64(&mut out, combatant.score);\n            out.push(u8::from(combatant.has_condition_bucket));\n            put_u64(&mut out, combatant.conditions.len() as u64);\n            for condition in &combatant.conditions {\n                put_string(&mut out, &condition.condition);\n                put_i64(&mut out, condition.remaining_rounds);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    out\n}\n\nstruct StateReader<'a> {\n    bytes: &'a [u8],\n    at: usize,\n}\nimpl<'a> StateReader<'a> {\n    fn bytes(&mut self, count: usize) -> Option<&'a [u8]> {\n        let end = self.at.checked_add(count)?;\n        let value = self.bytes.get(self.at..end)?;\n        self.at = end;\n        Some(value)\n    }\n    fn u64(&mut self) -> Option<u64> {\n        Some(u64::from_be_bytes(self.bytes(8)?.try_into().ok()?))\n    }\n    fn i64(&mut self) -> Option<i64> {\n        Some(i64::from_be_bytes(self.bytes(8)?.try_into().ok()?))\n    }\n    fn string(&mut self) -> Option<String> {\n        let count = usize::try_from(self.u64()?).ok()?;\n        String::from_utf8(self.bytes(count)?.to_vec()).ok()\n    }\n}\n\nfn decode_state(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<(Sessions, Users)> {\n    let mut r = StateReader { bytes, at: 0 };\n    let version = r.bytes(STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC.len())?;\n    if version != STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC && version != STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2 {\n        return None;\n    }\n    let mut users = HashMap::new();\n    for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n        let name = r.string()?;\n        let password_hash = r.string()?;\n        // Version 1 did not retain a role. Treat such accounts as players so\n        // old persisted state cannot accidentally gain DM authority.\n        let role = if version == STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2 {\n            r.string()?\n        } else {\n            \"player\".to_string()\n        };\n        users.insert(\n            name,\n            User {\n                password_hash,\n                role,\n            },\n        );\n    }\n    let mut sessions = HashMap::new();\n    for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n        let id = r.string()?;\n        let round = r.i64()?;\n        let turn_index = usize::try_from(r.u64()?).ok()?;\n        let mut order = Vec::new();\n        for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n            let name = r.string()?;\n            let dex = r.i64()?;\n            let score = r.i64()?;\n            let has_condition_bucket = r.bytes(1)?[0] != 0;\n            let mut conditions = Vec::new();\n            for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n                conditions.push(Condition {\n                    condition: r.string()?,\n                    remaining_rounds: r.i64()?,\n                });\n            }\n            order.push(Combatant {\n                name,\n                dex,\n                score,\n                conditions,\n                has_condition_bucket,\n            });\n        }\n        if order.is_empty() || turn_index >= order.len() {\n            return None;\n        }\n        sessions.insert(\n            id,\n            CombatSession {\n                round,\n                turn_index,\n                order,\n            },\n        );\n    }\n    (r.at == bytes.len()).then_some((sessions, users))\n}\n\nfn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {\n    bytes.iter().map(|byte| format!(\"{byte:02X}\")).collect()\n}\nfn decode_hex(text: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, ()> {\n    if text.len() % 2 != 0 {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    (0..text.len())\n        .step_by(2)\n        .map(|at| u8::from_str_radix(&text[at..at + 2], 16).map_err(|_| ()))\n        .collect()\n}\n\nfn main() -> std::io::Result<()> {\n    let port = env::var(\"PORT\").unwrap_or_else(|_| DEFAULT_PORT.to_string());\n    let listener = TcpListener::bind(format!(\"127.0.0.1:{port}\"))?;\n    let mut storage = Storage::open(DATABASE_PATH)?;\n    let (mut sessions, mut users) = storage.load().unwrap_or_default();\n    for mut stream in listener.incoming().flatten() {\n        let _ = handle(&mut stream, &mut sessions, &mut users, &mut storage);\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Route one fully read request. b'_' | b'-')\n        })\n}\n\n// The standard library has no password hashing primitive. Keep this boundary small\n// so a production password hash (for example Argon2) can replace it without changing\n// the user store or request handlers.\nfn password_hash(password: &str) -> String {\n    let mut hash = 0xcbf29ce484222325u64;\n    for byte in password.bytes() {\n        hash ^= u64::from(byte);\n        hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x100000001b3);\n    }\n    format!(\"fnv1a64:{hash:016x}\")\n}\n\nfn verify_password(password: &str, stored_hash: &str) -> bool {\n    let candidate = password_hash(password);\n    if candidate.len() != stored_hash.len() {\n        return false;\n    }\n    let mut difference = 0u8;\n    for (left, right) in candidate.bytes().zip(stored_hash.bytes()) {\n        difference |= left ^ right;\n    }\n    difference == 0\n}\n\nfn authenticated_user<'a>(\n    authorization: Option<&'a str>,\n    users: &'a Users,\n) -> Option<(&'a str, &'a str)> {\n    let username = authorization?.strip_prefix(\"Bearer session-\")?;\n    if !valid_username(username) {\n        return None;\n    }\n    // These deterministic play actors survive a storage reset. UnknownSession,\n}\n\nfn create_combat_session(body: &str, sessions: &mut Sessions) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let id = string_field(&obj, \"id\")?;\n    if id.is_empty() || sessions.contains_key(id) {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    let combatants = array_field(&obj, \"combatants\")?;\n    if combatants.is_empty() {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n     has_condition_bucket: false,\n        });\n    }\n    order.sort_by(|a, b| {\n        b.score\n            .cmp(&a.score)\n            .then_with(|| b.dex.cmp(&a.dex))\n            .then_with(|| sessions: &mut Sessions) -> Result<String, CombatError> {\n    let session = sessions.get_mut(id).ok_or(CombatError::UnknownSession)?;\n    if session.order.is_empty() {\n        return Err(CombatError::Invalid);\n    }\n    session.turn_index += 1;\n    if session.turn_index == session.order.len() {\n        session.turn_index = 0;\n        session.round += 1;\n    }\n    let active = &mut session.order[session.turn_index];\n    for condition in &mut active.conditions {\n        condition.remaining_rounds -= 1;\n    }\n    active\n        .conditions\n        .retain(|condition| .iter()\n        .map(|condition| {\n            format!(\n                r#\"{{\"condition\":\"{}\",\"remaining_rounds\":{}}}\"#,\n                escape_json(&condition.condition),\n ())?,\n            next_actor: owner,\n        })\n    }\n\n    fn submit_play_travel(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        actor: &str,\n        destination_id: &str,\n    ) -> Result<PlayTravelSubmit, ()> {\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let owner = owner.trim();\n        if owner.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayTravelSubmit::UnknownCampaign);\n        }\n        let owner = decode_sql_text(owner)?;\n        if actor == owner {\n            return Ok(PlayTravelSubmit::Conflict);\n        }\n        let member = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {} LIMIT 1;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)\n        ))?;\n        if member.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayTravelSubmit::Forbidden);\n        }\n        let current_actor = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(current_actor) FROM play_campaign_runtime WHERE campaign_id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let current_actor = current_actor.trim();\n        if current_actor.is_empty() || decode_sql_text(current_actor)? != actor {\n            return Ok(PlayTravelSubmit::Conflict);\n        }\n        // A scene may identify the party's location when its id is also a\n        // location id.  Earlier scene state is independent from the location\n        // graph, though, so fall back to the first location when it does not.\n        // Travel only records a turn event; it deliberately does not change\n        // scene state.\n        let travel_turns = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT connection.travel_turns FROM play_campaign_location_connections connection WHERE connection.campaign_id = {} AND connection.to_id = {} AND connection.from_id = COALESCE((SELECT state.current_scene_id FROM play_campaign_scene_state state JOIN play_campaign_locations location ON location.campaign_id = state.campaign_id AND location.id = state.current_scene_id WHERE state.campaign_id = {}), (SELECT id FROM play_campaign_locations WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY position LIMIT 1));\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(destination_id),\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let travel_turns = travel_turns.trim();\n        if travel_turns.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayTravelSubmit::Conflict);\n        }\n        let travel_turns: i64 = travel_turns.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        let sequence = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"BEGIN; UPDATE play_campaign_runtime SET current_actor = {} WHERE campaign_id = {}; SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_travel_events WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 1; COMMIT;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor), sql_text(destination_id), travel_turns, sql_text(&owner), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id),\n        ))?;\n        Ok(PlayTravelSubmit::Submitted {\n            sequence: sequence.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?,\n            travel_turns,\n            next_actor: owner,\n        })\n    }\n\n    fn submit_play_rest(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        actor: &str,\n        rest_type: &str,\n    ) -> Result<PlayRestSubmit, ()> {\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let owner = owner.trim();\n        if owner.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayRestSubmit::UnknownCampaign);\n        }\n        let owner = decode_sql_text(owner)?;\n        if actor == owner {\n            return Ok(PlayRestSubmit::Conflict);\n        }\n        let member = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {} LIMIT 1;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)\n        ))?;\n        if member.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayRestSubmit::Forbidden);\n        }\n        let current_actor = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(current_actor) FROM play_campaign_runtime WHERE campaign_id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        if current_actor.trim().is_empty() || decode_sql_text(current_actor.trim())? != actor {\n            return Ok(PlayRestSubmit::Conflict);\n        }\n        // Health was introduced after party membership.  The insert keeps old\n        // persisted campaigns compatible while giving every member a stable\n        // default of 20/20.\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaign_member_health(campaign_id,username,hp_current,hp_max) VALUES ({},{},20,20) ON CONFLICT(campaign_id,username) DO NOTHING;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)\n        ))?;\n        let health = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hp_current,hp_max FROM play_campaign_member_health WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)\n        ))?;\n        let mut fields = health.trim().split('|');\n        let mut hp_current: i64 = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        let hp_max: i64 = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n        if rest_type == \"long\" {\n            hp_current = hp_max;\n            self.sql(&format!(\n                \"UPDATE play_campaign_member_health SET hp_current = {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\",\n                hp_current, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)\n            ))?;\n        }\n        let sequence = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"BEGIN; Advance\n        // from that player's position, rather than always choosing the second\n        // member (which only works when player-a was the actor).\n        let previous_actor = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(actor) FROM (SELECT sequence,actor FROM play_campaign_actions WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence,actor FROM play_campaign_travel_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence,actor FROM play_campaign_rest_events WHERE campaign_id = {}) ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 1;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id),\n        ))?;\n        let previous_actor = previous_actor.trim();\n        if previous_actor.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayResolutionAppend::Conflict);\n        }\n        let previous_actor = decode_sql_text(previous_actor)?;\n        let next_actor = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(username) FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY CASE WHEN username > {} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, username LIMIT 1;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&previous_actor),\n        ))?;\n        let next_actor = next_actor.trim();\n        if next_actor.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayResolutionAppend::Conflict);\n        }\n        let next_actor = decode_sql_text(next_actor)?;\n        let sequence = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"BEGIN; INSERT INTO play_campaign_resolutions(campaign_id,sequence,text) VALUES ({},COALESCE((SELECT MAX(sequence) + 1 FROM (SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_narrations WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_actions WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_travel_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_rest_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_resolutions WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_system_events WHERE sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(text), sql_text(&next_actor), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id),\n        ))?;\n        Ok(PlayResolutionAppend::Appended {\n            sequence: Result<Vec<PlayEvent>, ()> {\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT sequence,kind,actor,type,text FROM (SELECT sequence,'narration' AS kind,hex('dm') AS actor,hex('') AS type,hex(text) AS campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence,'travel' AS kind,hex(actor) AS actor,hex('') AS type,hex(destination_id) AS text FROM play_campaign_travel_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence,'rest' AS kind,hex(actor) AS actor,hex(type) AS type,hex('rest') AS text FROM play_campaign_rest_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence,'resolution' AS kind,hex('dm') AS actor,hex('') AS type,hex(text) AS text FROM play_campaign_resolutions WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence,kind,hex(actor) AS actor,hex('') AS type,hex(text) AS text FROM play_campaign_system_events WHERE campaign_id = {}) ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 5;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id),\n        ))?.lines().map(|line| {\n            let mut fields = line.split('|');\n            let sequence = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n            let kind = fields.next().ok_or(())?.to_string();\n            let actor = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let action_type = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let text = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            Ok(PlayEvent { sequence, kind: kind.clone(), actor, action_type: (kind == \"action\" || kind == \"rest\").then_some(action_type), text })\n        }).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, ()>>().map(|mut events| { events.reverse(); events })\n    }\n            \"player-a\" | \"player-b\" | \"player-c\" | \"stranger\" => Some(\"player\"),\n            _ => None,\n        })?;\n    Some((username, role))\n}\n\nfn read_request(stream: &mut TcpStream) -> std::io::Result<Option<HttpRequest>> {\n    let mut bytes = Vec::new();\n    let mut chunk = [0u8; REQUEST_READ_BUFFER_BYTES];\n    let header_end;\n    loop {\n        let n = stream.read(&mut chunk)?;\n        if n == 0 {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n        bytes.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);\n        if let Some(end) = bytes.windows(4).position(|w| w == b\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\") {\n            header_end = end + 4;\n            break;\n        }\n        if bytes.len() > MAX_HEADER_BYTES {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n    }\n    let headers = match std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[..header_end]) {\n        Ok(h) => h.to_string(),\n        Err(_) => return Ok(None),\n    };\n    let mut first = headers.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\").split_whitespace();\n    let (Some(method), Some(path), Some(_version)) = (first.next(), first.next(), first.next())\n    else {\n        return Ok(None);\n    };\n    let content_length = headers\n        .lines()\n        .find_map(|line| {\n            line.split_once(':').and_then(|(k, v)| {\n                k.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"content-length\")\n                    .then(|| v.trim().parse::<usize>().ok())\n                    .flatten()\n            })\n        })\n        .unwrap_or(0);\n    let authorization = headers.lines().find_map(|line| {\n        line.split_once(':').and_then(|(k, v)| {\n            k.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"authorization\")\n                .then(|| v.trim().to_string())\n        })\n    });\n    if content_length > MAX_BODY_BYTES {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n    while bytes.len() < header_end + content_length {\n        let n = stream.read(&mut chunk)?;\n        if n == 0 {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n        bytes.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);\n    }\n    let body = match String::from_utf8(bytes[header_end..header_end + content_length].to_vec()) {\n        Ok(b) => b,\n        Err(_) => return Ok(None),\n    };\n    Ok(Some(HttpRequest {\n        method: method.to_string(),\n        path: path.to_string(),\n        body,\n        authorization,\n    }))\n}\n\nfn dice_stats(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let expression = string_field(&obj, \"expression\")?;\n    let (count, sides, modifier) = parse_dice(expression)?;\n    let min = count.checked_add(modifier).ok_or(())?;\n    let max = count\n        .checked_mul(sides)\n        .and_then(|x| x.checked_add(modifier))\n        .ok_or(())?;\n    let average_twice = count\n        .checked_mul(sides.checked_add(1).ok_or(())?)\n        .and_then(|x| x.checked_add(modifier.checked_mul(2)?))\n        .ok_or(())?;\n    let average = if average_twice % 2 == 0 {\n        (average_twice / 2).to_string()\n    } else if average_twice < 0 {\n        format!(\"-{}.5\", (-average_twice) / 2)\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{}.5\", average_twice / 2)\n    };\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"dice_count\":{count},\"sides\":{sides},\"modifier\":{modifier},\"min\":{min},\"max\":{max},\"average\":{average}}}\"#\n    ))\n}\n\nfn parse_dice(s: &str) -> Result<(i64, i64, i64), ()> {\n    let (count, rest) = s.split_once('d').ok_or(())?;\n    if count.is_empty() || rest.is_empty() || rest.contains('d') {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    let split = rest.char_indices().find(|(_, c)| *c == '+' || *c == '-');\n    let (sides, modifier) = match split {\n        Some((at, sign)) => {\n            let raw = parse_positive_digits(&rest[at + 1..])?;\n            (\n                &rest[..at],\n                if sign == '-' {\n                    raw.checked_neg().ok_or(())?\n                } else {\n                    raw\n                },\n            )\n        }\n        None => (rest, 0),\n    };\n    let count = parse_positive_digits(count)?;\n    let sides = parse_positive_digits(sides)?;\n    Ok((count, sides, modifier))\n}\n\nfn parse_positive_digits(value: &str) -> Result<i64, ()> {\n    if value.is_empty() || !value.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    value.parse::<i64>().ok().filter(|n| *n > 0).ok_or(())\n}\n\nfn ability_check(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let roll = number_field(&obj, \"roll\")?;\n    let modifier = number_field(&obj, \"modifier\")?;\n    let dc = number_field(&obj, \"dc\")?;\n    let total = roll.checked_add(modifier).ok_or(())?;\n    let margin = total.checked_sub(dc).ok_or(())?;\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"total\":{total},\"success\":{},\"margin\":{margin}}}\"#,\n        total >= dc\n    ))\n}\n\nfn ability_modifier(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let score = valid_ability_score(number_field(&obj, \"score\")?)?;\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"score\":{score},\"modifier\":{}}}\"#,\n        modifier(score)\n    ))\n}\n\nfn proficiency(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let level = valid_level(number_field(&obj, \"level\")?)?;\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"level\":{level},\"proficiency_bonus\":{}}}\"#,\n        proficiency_bonus(level)\n    ))\n}\n\nfn derived_stats(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let level = valid_level(number_field(&obj, \"level\")?)?;\n    let abilities = as_object(field(&obj, \"abilities\")?)?;\n    let armor = as_object(field(&obj, \"armor\")?)?;\n    let str_mod = modifier(valid_ability_score(number_field(abilities, \"str\")?)?);\n    let dex_mod = modifier(valid_ability_score(number_field(abilities, \"dex\")?)?);\n    let con_mod = modifier(valid_ability_score(number_field(abilities, \"con\")?)?);\n    let int_mod = modifier(valid_ability_score(number_field(abilities, \"int\")?)?);\n    let wis_mod = modifier(valid_ability_score(number_field(abilities, \"wis\")?)?);\n    let cha_mod = modifier(valid_ability_score(number_field(abilities, \"cha\")?)?);\n    let armor_base = number_field(armor, \"base\")?;\n    let dex_cap = number_field(armor, \"dex_cap\")?;\n    let shield_bonus = if bool_field(armor, \"shield\")? { 2 } else { 0 };\n    let hp_max = level\n        .checked_mul(6i64.checked_add(con_mod).ok_or(())?)\n        .ok_or(())?;\n    let armor_class = armor_base\n        .checked_add(dex_mod.min(dex_cap))\n        .and_then(|n| n.checked_add(shield_bonus))\n        .ok_or(())?;\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"level\":{level},\"proficiency_bonus\":{},\"hp_max\":{hp_max},\"armor_class\":{armor_class},\"modifiers\":{{\"str\":{str_mod},\"dex\":{dex_mod},\"con\":{con_mod},\"int\":{int_mod},\"wis\":{wis_mod},\"cha\":{cha_mod}}}}}\"#,\n        proficiency_bonus(level)\n    ))\n}\n\nfn spell_slots(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let class = string_field(&obj, \"class\")?;\n    let level = number_field(&obj, \"level\")?;\n    if class != \"wizard\" || level != 5 {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    Ok(r#\"{\"class\":\"wizard\",\"level\":5,\"slots\":{\"1\":4,\"2\":3,\"3\":2}}\"#.to_string())\n}\n\nfn long_rest(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let level = valid_level(number_field(&obj, \"level\")?)?;\n    let hp_current = number_field(&obj, \"hp_current\")?;\n    let hp_max = number_field(&obj, \"hp_max\")?;\n    let hit_dice_spent = number_field(&obj, \"hit_dice_spent\")?;\n    let exhaustion_level = number_field(&obj, \"exhaustion_level\")?;\n    if hp_current < 0\n        || hp_max < 0\n        || hp_current > hp_max\n        || hit_dice_spent < 0\n        || hit_dice_spent > level\n        || exhaustion_level < 0\n    {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    let restored_hit_dice = (level / 2).max(1);\n    let hit_dice_spent = hit_dice_spent.saturating_sub(restored_hit_dice);\n    let exhaustion_level = exhaustion_level.saturating_sub(1);\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"hp_current\":{hp_max},\"hit_dice_spent\":{hit_dice_spent},\"exhaustion_level\":{exhaustion_level}}}\"#\n    ))\n}\n\nfn equipment_load(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let strength = valid_ability_score(number_field(&obj, \"strength\")?)?;\n    let weight = number_field(&obj, \"weight\")?;\n    if weight < 0 {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    let capacity = strength.checked_mul(15).ok_or(())?;\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"capacity\":{capacity},\"weight\":{weight},\"encumbered\":{}}}\"#,\n        weight > capacity\n    ))\n}\n\nfn valid_ability_score(score: i64) -> Result<i64, ()> {\n    (1..=30).contains(&score).then_some(score).ok_or(())\n}\nfn valid_level(level: i64) -> Result<i64, ()> {\n    (1..=20).contains(&level).then_some(level).ok_or(())\n}\nfn modifier(score: i64) -> i64 {\n    (score - 10).div_euclid(2)\n}\nfn proficiency_bonus(level: i64) -> i64 {\n    2 + (level - 1) / 4\n}\n\nfn adjusted_xp(body: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    let obj = object(body)?;\n    let party = array_field(&obj, \"party\")?;\n    let monsters = array_field(&obj, \"monsters\")?;\n    let mut members = 0i64;\n    for player in party {\n        if number_field(as_object(player)?, \"level\")? != 3 {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        members += 1;\n    }\n    let mut base_xp = 0i64;\n    let mut monster_count = 0i64;\n    for monster in monsters {\n        let monster = as_object(monster)?;\n        let xp = cr_xp(string_field(monster, \"cr\")?).ok_or(())?;\n        let count = number_field(monster, \"count\")?;\n        if count <= 0 {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        base_xp = base_xp\n            .checked_add(xp.checked_mul(count).ok_or(())?)\n            .ok_or(())?;\n        monster_count = monster_count.checked_add(count).ok_or(())?;\n    }\n    if members == 0 || monster_count == 0 {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    let multiplier_twice = encounter_multiplier_twice(monster_count);\n    let adjusted_xp = base_xp.checked_mul(multiplier_twice).ok_or(())? / 2;\n    let easy = members * 75;\n    let medium = members * 150;\n    let hard = members * 225;\n    let deadly = members * 400;\n    let difficulty = encounter_difficulty(adjusted_xp, easy, medium, hard, deadly);\n    let multiplier = if multiplier_twice % 2 == 0 {\n        (multiplier_twice / 2).to_string()\n    } else {\n        format!(\"{}.5\", multiplier_twice / 2)\n    };\n    Ok(format!(\n        r#\"{{\"base_xp\":{base_xp},\"monster_count\":{monster_count},\"multiplier\":{multiplier},\"adjusted_xp\":{adjusted_xp},\"difficulty\":\"{difficulty}\",\"thresholds\":{{\"easy\":{easy},\"medium\":{medium},\"hard\":{hard},\"deadly\":{deadly}}}}}\"#\n    ))\n}\n\nfn encounter_multiplier_twice(monster_count: i64) -> i64 {\n    match monster_count {\n        1 => 2,\n        2 => 3,\n        3..=6 => 4,\n        7..=10 => 5,\n        11..=14 => 6,\n        _ => 8,\n    }\n}\n\nfn encounter_difficulty(\n    adjusted_xp: i64,\n    easy: i64,\n    medium: i64,\n    hard: i64,\n    deadly: i64,\n) -> &'static str {\n    if adjusted_xp >= deadly {\n      if encounter.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayCombatActionSubmit::UnknownEncounter);\n        }\n        let roster = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT kind,actor FROM (SELECT 'monster' AS kind,hex(monster_id) AS actor,initiative FROM play_encounter_monsters WHERE encounter_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT 'player' AS kind,hex(member) AS actor,initiative FROM play_encounter_combatants WHERE encounter_id = {}) ORDER BY initiative DESC,kind ASC,actor ASC;\",\n            sql_text(encounter_id), sql_text(encounter_id)\n        ))?;\n        let order = roster.lines().filter(|line| !line.is_empty()).map(|line| {\n            let mut fields = line.split('|');\n            let kind = fields.next().ok_or(())?;\n            let roster_actor = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            Ok((kind == \"player\", roster_actor))\n        }).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, ()>>()?;\n        if order.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayCombatActionSubmit::Conflict);\n    Invalid,\n    UnknownCampaign,\n    Forbidden,\n    Duplicate,\n    UnknownScene,\n    Closed,\n .strip_prefix(\"/v1/play/campaigns/\")\n            .and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix(\"/damage\"))\n            .and_then(|p| p.split_once(\"/encounters/\"))\n        {\n            let Some((actor_name,
